Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 4: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same? Yes. Skip line 5; enter the amount from line 2 on line 6." executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.deepseek.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 159 partial records): the reply has no line under this printed marker (missing), so nothing was executed. The prompt says a marker shown with a line id in parentheses must be written exactly so; the reply wrote printed "6" for eic.line6, so the comparison finds no candidate for "6 (eic.line6)", and eic.line6 is also among the reply's invalid lines. Project stands.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.eligible | eic.died_2025 | - |
| eic.credit | eic.died_2025 | - |
| eic.check_27c | eic.died_2025 | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    note: "The clergy/church-employee adjustment to line 1 is not supported by the closed input interface; line 1z is used directly."
  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    note: "No Medicaid waiver payment amount is in the closed input interface; within the supplied scope this amount is zero."
  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}
  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    note: "No nontaxable combat pay amount is in the closed input interface; within the supplied scope this amount is zero."
  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}
  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_1e, eic.h_wsb_2c, eic.h_wsb_3, eic.earned_income]}
  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {param: ss.zero}}
    note: "Floored at zero. The IRS STOP for line 4b zero or less is carried into the credit eligibility gate."
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}
  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line1_over_limit, {const: true}]}, then: {def: eic, bind: {}, line: eic.h_zero}}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line1_over_limit, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_table_earned}]}
  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}
  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If you have no qualifying children who have valid SSNs, is the amount on line 3 less than $10,620 ($17,730 if married filing jointly)? If you have 1 or more qualifying children who have valid SSNs, is the amount on line 3 less than $23,350 ($30,470 if married filing jointly)? If yes, leave line 5 blank and enter the amount from line 2 on line 6. If no, look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table and enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_below, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line2}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_below, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_line3_lookup}]}
  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line2}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_line6_smaller}]}
  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [s1.has_sc, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.clergy, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "Uses the available Schedule C aggregate. No separate self-employment/Schedule SE screening input is in the closed interface."
  - id: eic.eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You are eligible for the earned income credit after Steps 1 through 5."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_below, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [sa.excluded_income, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_investment_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_income_ok, {const: true}]}]}
    refuses: [eic.died_2025]
    note: "The Step 2 investment-income branch for Form 4797 filers is not separately supported; investment income over the limit is treated as ineligible. Nonresident-alien status is not separately supplied."
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.eligible, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_credit_computed}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.eligible, {const: false}]}, then: {def: eic, bind: {}, line: eic.h_zero}}]}
    note: "All STOP conditions are carried to this result as zero. Worksheet B is used when the available self-employment aggregate requires it."
  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27c."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.eligible, {const: false}]}
    note: "The voluntary election not to claim the EIC is not supplied by the closed interface; this records only the ineligible STOP cases."
  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}, {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}]}]}
    note: "Uses the main-home input as the available reading of the requirement that the qualifying child lived in the United States for more than half of 2025."
  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You are a minister, member of a religious order who has not taken a vow of poverty, or a Christian Science practitioner filing Schedule SE."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [{const: 0}, {const: 1}]}
    note: "No supplied input distinguishes clergy or church employees. Within the closed input interface this is set false rather than inventing an input."
  - id: eic.h_child_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of EIC qualifying children with valid SSNs (helper)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}}
  - id: eic.h_mfs_hoh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing separately or head of household (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_age_qualified
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of taxpayers or spouses age 25 through 64 at the end of 2025 (helper)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.one, where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "taxpayer"}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "spouse"}]}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}]}}
  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "At least one taxpayer or spouse is age 25 through 64 (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_age_qualified, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_ssn_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "All persons have an SSN valid for EIC purposes (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_cap_gain_floor
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 line 7a capital gain or loss, with a loss entered as zero (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line7a, take: {param: ss.zero}}
  - id: eic.h_investment_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Investment income from Form 1040 lines 2a, 2b, 3b and 7a (helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_cap_gain_floor]}
  - id: eic.h_investment_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Investment income is not more than $11,950 (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_investment_income, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}
  - id: eic.h_p_limit0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC income limit, no children, other filing statuses (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}
  - id: eic.h_p_limit0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC income limit, no children, married filing jointly (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_p_limit1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC income limit, one child, other filing statuses (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}
  - id: eic.h_p_limit1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC income limit, one child, married filing jointly (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_p_limit2_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC income limit, two children, other filing statuses (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}
  - id: eic.h_p_limit2_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC income limit, two children, married filing jointly (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}
  - id: eic.h_p_limit3_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC income limit, three or more children, other filing statuses (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}
  - id: eic.h_p_limit3_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC income limit, three or more children, married filing jointly (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}
  - id: eic.h_income_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC income limit for the number of qualifying children and filing status (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}, then: eic.h_p_limit3_joint}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}, then: eic.h_p_limit3_other}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}, then: eic.h_p_limit2_joint}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}, then: eic.h_p_limit2_other}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}, then: eic.h_p_limit1_joint}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}, then: eic.h_p_limit1_other}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}, then: eic.h_p_limit0_joint}, {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}]}, then: eic.h_p_limit0_other}]}
  - id: eic.h_agi_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 line 11b is less than the EIC income limit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_income_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_separated_rule_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The Step 3 separated-spouse filing-status sequence does not stop the credit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfs_hoh, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_step3_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 does not stop the credit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_separated_rule_ok, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_step4_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 does not stop the credit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfs_hoh, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}, {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}]}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_children_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The Step 3 or Step 4 child/no-child branch does not stop the credit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step3_ok, {const: true}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_ok, {const: true}]}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_income_for_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income used for the Step 5 or Worksheet B limit (helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}, then: eic.wsb_6}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}, then: eic.earned_income}]}
  - id: eic.h_income_positive_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income is positive where the self-employment worksheet requires it (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}, {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {param: ss.zero}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_income_below_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income is less than the EIC income limit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.h_income_for_limit, eic.h_income_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_income_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The Step 5 earned-income limit does not stop the credit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_income_positive_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_income_below_limit, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_line1_over_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 1 is at or above the EIC income limit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.line1, eic.h_income_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_earned_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 1, no qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_earned_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 1, one qualifying child (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_earned_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 1, two qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_earned_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 1, three or more qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_table_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 1 using the qualifying-child table (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_earned_3}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_earned_2}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_earned_1}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_earned_0}]}
  - id: eic.h_line3_over_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 is at or above the EIC income limit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_income_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_agi_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 3, no qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_agi_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 3, one qualifying child (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_agi_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 3, two qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_agi_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 3, three or more qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_table_agi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table lookup for line 3 using the qualifying-child table (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_agi_3}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_agi_2}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_agi_1}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_agi_0}]}
  - id: eic.h_line3_lookup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 EIC Table amount after the out-of-range zero gate (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line3_over_limit, {const: true}]}, then: {def: eic, bind: {}, line: eic.h_zero}}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line3_over_limit, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_table_agi}]}
  - id: eic.h_line5_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 is below the Worksheet A line 5 threshold (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.other}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.other}]}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_line6_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of Worksheet A lines 2 and 5 (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line2, eic.line5]}
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero-valued money helper"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_1b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 1b: Schedule SE Part I lines 4b and 5a (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [line4b, line5b]}
    note: "Schedule SE line 5a is not in the catalog; line 5b is the available Schedule SE line 5 amount."
  - id: eic.h_wsb_1c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 1c: combine lines 1a and 1b (helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [line3, eic.h_wsb_1b]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_1e
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 1e: subtract line 1d from line 1c (helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.h_wsb_1c, take: line13}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_2c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 2c: combine lines 2a and 2b (helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s1.line3}
    note: "No Schedule F or K-1 amounts are in the closed interface; the available Schedule 1 business-income aggregate is used."
  - id: eic.h_wsb_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 3: statutory employee Schedule C amount (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    note: "No statutory-employee Schedule C amount is in the closed interface; within the supplied scope this amount is zero."
  - id: eic.h_wsb_6_over_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 6 is at or above the EIC income limit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.wsb_6, eic.h_income_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_wsb_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 6, no qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_wsb_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 6, one qualifying child (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_wsb_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 6, two qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_wsb_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 6, three or more qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_table_wsb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 6 using the qualifying-child table (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_wsb_3}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_wsb_2}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_wsb_1}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_wsb_0}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7 after the out-of-range zero gate (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_6_over_limit, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_zero}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_6_over_limit, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_table_wsb}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 8: Form 1040 line 11b (helper)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 9: lines 8 and 6 are the same (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_8, eic.wsb_6]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_10_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 8 is below the line 10 threshold (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.h_wsb_8, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.h_wsb_8, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.other}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.h_wsb_8, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}]}]}, {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.h_wsb_8, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.other}]}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_wsb8_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 8, no qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.h_wsb_8, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_wsb8_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 8, one qualifying child (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.h_wsb_8, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_wsb8_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 8, two qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.h_wsb_8, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tbl_wsb8_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 8, three or more qualifying children (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.h_wsb_8, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_table_wsb8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B EIC Table lookup for line 8 using the qualifying-child table (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_wsb8_3}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_wsb8_2}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_wsb8_1}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_child_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_tbl_wsb8_0}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_8_over_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 8 is at or above the EIC income limit (helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_wsb_8, eic.h_income_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_8_lookup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 8 EIC Table amount after the out-of-range zero gate (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_8_over_limit, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_zero}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_8_over_limit, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_table_wsb8}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 10 after the below-threshold choice (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_10_below, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_wsb_7}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_10_below, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_wsb_8_lookup}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_11_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of Worksheet B lines 7 and 10 (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.h_wsb_7, eic.h_wsb_10]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 11: earned income credit (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_9, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_wsb_7}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_9, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_wsb_11_smaller}]}
  - id: eic.h_credit_computed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC credit before the eligibility gate (helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_wsb_11}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}, then: eic.line6}]}
```
